                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. KT클라우드 1GW 로드맵과 글로벌 기가와트급 증설 및 피지컬 AI 결합</div>하이퍼스케일 거점 확보와 피지컬 AI 관제 도입이 결합되며 연산 인프라가 급격히 대형화되는 추세임. 국내에서는 KT클라우드가 2031년까지 전국 20여 곳에 1GW 규모 AIDC를 구축하는 로드맵을 발표함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=(AI픽) KT클라우드, AIDC 20여곳에 1GW 공급…AX 인프라 확대&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. KT는 5년간 6조 원을 투입해 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiWkFVX3lxTFBLMEc4VUpWemFEZ3pQTFFISmRBWHNaZ0xsdDc0cU5VMUZPVXVjQlRvX3B0R19VODdWNG1BMkZQQ1Y1d3ktUHhDQm1wMWZrRmdWd3hmQ2g1bF85Zw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a> 140kW급 초고전력 랙을 도입하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiWEFVX3lxTE54Z2RGbTd3dDgtLWpjZzZUY3RPd1ZIRVEyb01hZHVTeVNjVTVjNEcxRUhOazBVOTB3WFdaRjgxNkdiQW5nRVJEYWp6bF9jTkJiSUtneEFQQ0fSAVhBVV95cUxOeGdkRm03d3Q4LS1qY2c2VGN0T3dWSEVRMm9NYWR1U3lTY1U1YzRHMUVITmswVTkwd1hXWkY4MTZHYkFuZ0VSRGFqemxfY05CYklLZ3hBUENH?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a> 현대차 로봇 '스팟'을 활용한 무인 자율 관제 체계를 가동하기로 결정함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi2AFBVV95cUxPUGd6VzVOZlgydTBqNmlhTEd2SDZ0TngtZS1EaHgzRW9oTU5UdGx3d0hZOGYwb2dZWTVHZkxzQ1pXLVE5dG5Gbm5KVm5TSTNkYTZSako2ZnJCSzl0cGo3WGhETnBnUWROLXhGUDlFcFVIUDlZSmpHX04tYlRtTkdUbG1GRm9YSGN2WWMwSVRyVW1hTUxJRUJxbjQ1SkFEa0Y1VnJaZVRuRlVZdVotWFFJYmUyR1N2U0RXYlNkTDgtaWFFcFU0eFZwVkVtVDE3aklsS01hNXVDOHc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서도 통신사 주도의 확장이 이어져 캐나다 벨(Bell)이 서스캐처원 AI 센터를 1.2GW로 확대하고 <a href='https://www.datacenterdynamics.com/en/news/canadas-bell-to-triple-capacity-at-planned-saskatchewan-data-center-campus/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a> 최대 525억 캐나다 달러(약 52조 5,000억 원) 투자를 확정함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMidkFVX3lxTE4zbFJyOWFQYTRuU2NZZ0ZwUkxvR043aHY4TW95NUh3Q19tWFd4Ylc4TjU3emg3WXE4dkdweEQ5MGVNakhfWHg3RUtQbW9WN05tbHcxMkRMclZTN2tPWWtPTS1tQXJVSG8zdU03X0lzcjM1anJQTGc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a>. 삼성SDS 역시 구미에 60MW급 AI 데이터센터를 본격 착공하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE54TnFYRTVpOUtxQU16b3JKZHE0RE1FZ1dfM0FqVl9ENTc1b0VNUzduY2kzUXF5NTVJMjAzeHF6WTkzdm93WktmSXBqN2FCRjBzOWNoejV5Mmo3ZllBb1JjVmJaZEItYlA3ZEE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a> 지역 상생 협력을 가속화했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiWEFVX3lxTE0yLTFBZ2dDU0t4dDBYYUxKU01fem5ZVzVGVV9NNGI0MV9kaVR3UDdkV192aS11OW8zT3BRTV9DeFhDTXd0U1BuQ1l5RGxlVXE4X1Zic3BLRDY?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>, 국내 최초로 앤트로픽의 셀렉트 티어 파트너 자격을 획득해 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYEFVX3lxTE5zMkxYalh6NkRlNUJPY2szajJjNzEza2NYWUIwNDQ5UGpoSldTM0VHeGhtMXdxZEkxcVdiWDVwcGtBVlI2Q0JUUWQ3VXQyT19pOUpZRm5hdmtfVzFPVkcxVtIBeEFVX3lxTE44S2RPMGRYNVlrUzlkdXJzdFNFZFd3cGJGWEtzNGVLQ2hNZHcyRlV1TVhnNmZESF9MQ0NQSzNLNTU1ZmJKdXN5Qi1ZNjBzM1ZLVmlHWnE2NzR0RmdVeXhTYkRSTlFwYUx0aVpmSEdpbktoV3BOc2hVNQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a> 클라우드 연계 역량을 입증함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiYkFVX3lxTE13UGxCcjdWVlUxR2NtRVhTVmRjMjYtQTQ4QzlTdWg1bVNHSnVySHdhSVRtcmZXWE52QThCUzJoVElQVDI0a0NPQWVPUVFNZ09mNnFkN1Y2b2E0a0lITHE1UHd3?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>. 동시에 퓨리오사AI는 에퀴닉스 리스본 센터에 추론 서버를 배치하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTE0zMWJjVEJMR0NKZVNUQy1MOXJaVUlCbHIwbUQ4VWx5bFlyNUl6cVQ3MllkZjBvSkxtVEJuaXExS2FweXNfcHNMZWVabVRWWkJTclFPMHJmM08zV2dDTl9pSGt0cmJwb3FN?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a> 유럽 소버린 AI 영토 확장에 나섰으며 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=FuriosaAI Deploys Inference Servers at Equinix Lisbon Data Center, Targeting Europe's Sovereign AI Market&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a>, 크루소(Crusoe)는 퍼플렉시티 AI와 다년 클라우드 계약을 체결해 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiW0FVX3lxTE9kYThtV2M0NUlJendRWDVhWXNiZERuRFN6YjdWUHNsRU9KUnhmbUJrSWRwYVl2aEdJM0VKV1JVTUxRRVE4Y3EyTDEwenZKVW5nVEhWblM3MVRYWTA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 특화 연산 자원 공급을 공식화함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMi9gFBVV95cUxNYW1hS2RQMWowaGI5blN3UFFWWkRqcWh6M0k5Q01oLTdYckNTU2pHTFpkV1I0UFNHVGtQRU41UzRnRHNwQmloa1h4aUxTbFVNdEViTlM1WGFXeERXRm9PaVJ3cVZSM3dSTHhkVmEwQWZZOEJsM1dYcTlucF9nUzlQZnpDZ0FxODBSektiVXRqc041djA0ZlcxUDdJZV9VZHF0V19VSzdXa0lENEMzV01Ba0pEYjd6YTNfVUxxQWgzMzVIMnB4bnRabUVLYWpyUFZXN0kwb0FxYkhpanBxdUIyWDJQeHN1eG1VQkNicXBISHQzcU1zY2c?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. 걸프 공습 피해의 복구 불가 판정과 클라우드 물리 파괴에 맞선 DR 재편</div>군사 분쟁에 따른 물리적 피격이 현실화되며 글로벌 클라우드의 재해복구(DR) 안전판이 시험대에 직면함. 아마존 AWS는 이란의 공습으로 타격을 입은 바레인 리전과 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMizAFBVV95cUxPcGhFMHZVNGthdVl1bjYza3ZOWVpRY3ZMS1JmZkgxVnJTWGRhZFdFS3JfYk1nX2Y1Nzl3RlZDN21lWFA3b0xUU1E3UkdZR0hPQXNXdG5seEhsQ0hUR1gxVnZCWHFpTXdQdGhGQkFKTFd0cW80QUNBdnpXYzdOX25zZEJpN2lkOW1IWVlnc0RZNDk0cGgzSTlNYXFaMS1EcUJTM0ZUUjlhS0FLV0NrbGtsM3BKOVlFOWdFUi1sbGQtWmRqX3ZsODdPczRYRUw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> UAE 내 1개 가용영역의 데이터 접근 복구가 영구히 불가능하다고 공식 선언함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTFBSOEN1V1Ntdm5UMUVTZnNob0lUZEZZdkdpb0FsV3lnaUNzWHVtczVkeGNjNXFTY2ZDTUZxM3ZJM0I5ZVlwZEhJN0p5N1VtbmZselpMa094b3QxWmpIVTI5S1RWZEFHcml3blN4NHFkSGE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a>. 데이터가 복구 불능 상태에 빠지며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMilgFBVV95cUxOa0lNR0ZidGhtMGZQZ19iZGZTeHp0eTg2VjlWQVMzTjUwTDhPU3VnZFB6V2N1MlY1QnNWb05HelBVSDFPZy1VS1BSbjQ4THF2SDZ0M2dmcFZPcFBKMmtnMU9TQl92Tm13Ym4xU0QyY3R3N1d5VVhiaXhVa2FFaDBsV0pWX29CUWRjR3FIUjBuSFd1akt5VkE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a> 고객사들의 영구적 데이터 유실이 확인되자 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Amazon's AWS unable to restore Bahrain cloud facility, one UAE data zone after Iran war damage | World News&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[43]</a>. 반면 인허가 규제는 확산되어 오하이오주 리마시가 18개월 모라토리엄을 가결했고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMinAFBVV95cUxPYXYtT2JadFI5ZzRBS0xqZGVOTjVRVTk2a0Foa1loY2FvZDVCQ2oxZVFGNnl4Ui1vSUQ2V013N3ZkbzYyT3YtcVB1Zm5ORGItOWpWQklfQUNIRGZlSGY3OTNRUm5pQmQxbDNhb29FVzdvQTdOSDZWUjFYcHM1Qk1KOGVoZ1hVdXlUWFgtdFdWU193Z2k1YW9tTWZ6QTU?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[44]</a>, 캘리포니아 길로이 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMic0FVX3lxTFB4YmI1cE5SUXBkcmlRaVNWMlM3bkdCU3cwQkdobnpkNUZneDlRc2EtcHJTX1M1SktLSEFaYjFyZmdLS3pxaUN4UVdmR1dtc0Y4Tmd5R01YQXFVSUhULWhpZ0VLRTVMcTZnYVVVS2xxUldPdVnSAXhBVV95cUxNS2phTnp0ZVVZTjJkMU9rMlFNazh4M3hRcEd5Rkk2QUJjR0MzOVoxUEtyTjBCeC1UaFdrUUFITnk5TjRDN3JiMjhSUkx0RWNaOW0yUVFsVTI5NjA5X1JMbUtieEdxU2FONFZRMnczbm1SZDdiQ19lY1A?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[45]</a>, 오리건 우드번(120일) <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiogFBVV95cUxOR1g2aGpic0lzVkptLWhreFB0LWg3TlRLLWc3cXdvZjNxaGt0RmJaREMzY3FqeDdpZzVfVjI5dWlwY3gzcVJKYnRicWwwelBwUENKVmpzZ2tvN0Y3eTlLYTN0d2RtejhEUVc4UU1HdGFuSDh1a19fZkJ6Wmd0cG9VNzJhVFIwRDdTNXcwNnYzUENQeTJ4VW5YbWpVaktJbGdaRkE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[46]</a>, 미네소타 베미지(1년)가 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMipAFBVV95cUxOM2piWVdwRzFVRWllRUJRWGcxTWotZWVCNU5SQmpMWkRlVjhqMTBwS1RrV3NjeENubWg0SEZSTThObUJINnB6ZHkwQU9MV19kMUF0cDNuV2RWRDBqeWNuY25Cd1ljRmg1TGVYV2xjOVRsV3BPVlVIbzdoblp0c3lLa1NGMUVtcEVnOVVxTnBZcUlvWkZUcEJFWmhodnAzTFNBZDdHeA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[47]</a> 동결을 의결함. 뉴저지 시코커스 센터에서는 5,500갤런(약 2만 리터)의 발전용 디젤이 유출되며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMivAFBVV95cUxNMDZ5TVpVRUxDWFQtd1RwNVNpV29SZXdfY19jOUhzSUhaVEtSaVJaWHdvUmk0aFNicFpxeXd0MGktb3ByNHNNRnZ2RDBPNUFYU1AwY1Naa1NtNnlaRW5RRTJwTFI5U3JTQWhadTgxWm9teEhBWllUUnRxX29ZSTRId3JHbmdIVlZsR1ZZOUZZOXRHUmRKdW1TYmhYR25RWkptdFBkLWFVMjhfT29LaU5adnVkeEtaRGRMQnBpQw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[48]</a> 환경 반발이 격화됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMixwFBVV95cUxQQkszZFFJR2otM1ItQ1NfWmNlM0Foc1p2S3F5MTNmaktkUWJZS2J0OHNpOVVzYkFPSWUyTDRBTVY3amRqNTdaVEx1RUR3aTVwZm1XUEFlc3gzSkxrT1o1dU04Wjd4SEN2djl4TEpHcXpoeXdWVC1XbzI1b1RMOGtUU3Z3ZjFOQXFmc2pWTGhaZUJMME1BT2JDOE1RNTU3TnU4WG81TVgzX0EzN3ZXU3F2eGx2cmVIRmZTMmlkSnZ3Qy1zaTdwSHA0?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[49]</a>. 이에 도널드 트럼프 전 대통령은 젠슨 황 CEO와의 통화에서 AI 위험론을 사기로 규정하고 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Trump tells Nvidia's Jensen Huang that AI is 'bigger than the internet' and robot fears are a 'hoax'&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[50]</a> 데이터센터를 차세대 석유로 치켜세우며 규제 철폐를 주장했고 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Trump's Live Call with Jensen Huang: AI Doomsday Talk Is a Scam, Data Centers Are the Oil of the Next 20-30 Years&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[51]</a> 젠슨 황이 이에 전적으로 호응함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZ0FVX3lxTE1sNFFwZFFUelFDeXZPU1Vvb2RpYnR6Y1kwalQ2eVEyNlpILWx2MXpaNGVxX2x5ZE1nVUMzQ0FkQUlvaUl3cVZneV9yaW8zOGEwS2Z6OTVrS0JSVUx3S05EN2tNYWZrZkk?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[52]</a>.
